CORPORATE PROFILE
企業簡介
Chow Tai Fook Jewellery Group Limited (the “Group”; SEHK stock code: 1929) was listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of
Hong Kong in December 2011. The Group firmly upholds the vision: “To be the leading global jewellery brand that is a trusted lifetime
partner for every generation”, drawing on nearly a century of legacy and success.


Founded in 1929, the Group’s iconic brand “CHOW TAI FOOK” has become an emblem of tradition, celebrated for its bold designs and
an unwavering attention to detail. Building upon a rich heritage and a foundation of trust, the Group is not only widely recognised for
honouring traditions but also for fostering deep, meaningful connections with a diverse customer base through its exquisite jewellery.
The Group’s long-standing commitment to innovation and craftsmanship has been integral to its success over time and has become
synonymous with excellence, value and authenticity.


As a leading Chinese jeweller, the Group believes in blending contemporary cutting-edge designs with traditional techniques to create
jewellery that can be passed down from generation to generation. Every collection is thoughtfully conceived and crafted to reflect the
stories of our customers, celebrating the special moments in their lives. Committed to growing alongside our customers, the Group
embraces a spirit that aspires to inspire and captivate generations to come, weaving the story of CHOW TAI FOOK into the fabric of
their lives.


Offering a wide variety of products, services and channels, the Group’s brand portfolio comprises the CHOW TAI FOOK flagship brand
with curated retail experiences, and other individual brands including HEARTS ON FIRE, ENZO and MONOLOGUE.


The Group is committed to delivering sustainable long-term value creation for its stakeholders by enhancing the quality of earnings and
driving higher value growth. We operate an extensive omni-channel retail ecosystem, with a retail network across China and multiple
locations globally, complemented by a growing e-commerce business that further enhances our competitive edge.




周大福珠寶集團有限公司 (「 集團」；香港聯交所股份代號 ：1929) 於 2011 年 12 月在香港聯合交易所主板上市。集團承襲近百年光輝，
秉持「引領珠寶行業，相伴世代人生」的願景，昂首邁向新里程。


集團標誌性品牌「周大福」創立於 1929 年，透過別出心裁的設計和對細節的堅持，讓傳統成為經典。周大福珠寶集團承載著深厚的歷
史底蕴及堅實的品牌信賴基礎，一直視弘揚傳統文化為己任，並透過匠心打造的精緻飾品，與廣泛的顧客建立深厚的情感聯繫，成就
廣獲認同。集團對創新與工藝的恒久堅持是其取得成功的要素。時至今日，集團已成為了卓越品質、非凡價值和誠信可靠的代名詞。


周大福珠寶集團作為行業領先的中國珠寶商，確信透過現代創新設計揉合傳統工藝，能創造出代代相傳的珠寶首飾。我們每個產品系
列都經過巧妙構思、匠心製作，旨在述說不同顧客的故事，慶祝他們生命中每個特別時刻。我們將陪伴每一代的顧客一同成長，在他
們追尋幸福的道路上提供激勵和啟發，把「周大福」的品牌故事和產品編織到顧客的生活脈絡當中。


集團擁有廣泛的產品、服務及銷售渠道，品牌組合包括旗艦品牌「周大福」及精心策劃的零售體驗，以及 HEARTS ON FIRE、ENZO 與
MONOLOGUE 等其他個性品牌。


集 團 致 力 通 過 提 高 盈 利 質 量 和 推 動 更 高 價 值 的 增 長，為 不 同 持 份 者 創 造 可 持 續 的 長 期 價 值。我 們 構 建 了 龐 大 的 全 渠 道 零 售 生 態 系
統，門店網絡遍布中國和全球多個地區，同時擁有日益增長的電子商務業務，進一步強化競爭優勢。

4   A Snapshot of Chow Tai Fook Jewellery Group                                                     Chow Tai Fook Jewellery Group Limited
    周大福珠寶集團簡況                                                                                                    周大福珠寶集團有限公司
INTERIM RESULTS AT A GLANCE
中期業績概覽
KEY HIGHLIGHTS
• In the first half of FY2026 (“1HFY2026”), the Group demonstrated remarkable resilience and delivered solid results, supported by an
   improvement in consumer sentiment and a revival in jewellery spending across the Group’s key markets.

• We made steady progress in our brand transformation and product optimisation during the period. These strategic initiatives
   strengthened our operational efficiency and financial resilience, reaffirming our commitment to quality growth. Our Same Store Sales
   growth turned positive in the Chinese Mainland (the "Mainland") and Hong Kong and Macau during the period, validating our store
   optimisation strategy with a focus on store productivity.

• We further unveiled more new image stores in the Mainland and Macau during the period, targeting sophisticated affluent consumers
   with differentiated merchandise and a redefined retail experience. These premium stores have consistently delivered higher monthly
   sales than average stores in the same districts since their debut.

• Our key fixed-price signature collections sustained strong sales momentum and generated total sales of HK$3.4 billion during the
   period. In the Mainland, fixed-price jewellery contribution to Retail Sales Value notably increased to 31.8% from 27.4% in the same
   period last year, lending support to our gross profit margin. We launched our first High Jewellery Collection “Timeless Harmony” in
   June, marking a pivotal milestone in our brand transformation. This launch strategically reinforced our position as a leading global
   jewellery brand with unrivalled capability.

• The Group’s revenue remained stable at HK$38,986 million during the period. Gross profit margin was maintained above 30%, at a
   relatively high level in history, underpinned by an improved mix of higher-margin fixed-price jewellery and gold price appreciation.
   Operating profit remained resilient with a year-on-year growth of 0.7%. The operating profit margin achieved a five-year record high
   with 30 basis points expansion to 17.5%, thanks to an improved selling and distribution costs and general and administrative
   expenses ratio ("SG&A") amid effective store optimisation and disciplined cost management.

• The Group demonstrated solid recovery trajectory in 1HFY2026, reinforcing our confidence in sustaining our recovery through the
   second half. We shall remain agile to proactively refining our strategies to stay ahead in a dynamic environment.



主要重點
• 2026 財政年度上半年，受惠於消費氣氛改善及主要市場的珠寶消費回暖，集團展現卓越的韌性，交付出穩健業績。


• 期內，集團穩步推進品牌轉型及產品優化。這些策略性舉措不僅提升集團的營運效益及財務韌性，亦進一步確立我們邁向高質量
   增長的堅定承諾。期內，我們在中國內地 (「 內地」) 及港澳市場的同店銷售重拾增長，印證了我們聚焦門店生產力的優化策略發揮
   成效。


• 期內，我們繼續在內地及澳門開設新形象店，為高品味的富裕消費者提供差異化產品及重新定義的零售體驗。自開業以來，這些
   高端門店的每月銷售持續高於同區門店的平均水平。


• 本集團重點標誌性定價產品系列延續強勁的銷售勢頭，期內總銷售額達到 34 億港元。在內地市場，定價首飾對零售值的貢獻由去
   年同期 27.4% 顯著提升至 31.8%，為毛利率帶來支持。我們於 6 月隆重推出首個高級珠寶系列「和美東方」，標誌著品牌轉型的重要
   里程碑。此次發布策略性地以無可比擬的實力，鞏固周大福作為全球領先珠寶品牌的地位。


• 期內，集團營業額保持穩定，達 38,986 百萬港元。在高毛利定價首飾貢獻提升及黃金價格上漲的支持下，集團毛利率維持於 30%
   以上，處於歷史較高水平。經營溢利保持韌性，同比增長 0.7%。憑藉有效的門店優化及嚴謹的成本管理措施，銷售及行政開支比
   率得以改善，使經營溢利率擴闊 30 個點子至 17.5%，創下近五年新高。


• 集團 2026 財政年度上半年展現穩健復甦之勢，增強了我們對下半財年持續復甦的信心。我們將保持靈活並積極優化策略，以在多
   變的市場環境中保持領先。

• Profit attributable to shareholders stayed flat in 1HFY2026, as the            • 2026 財政年度上半年的股東應佔溢利維持平穩，儘管期
     increase in operating profit was offset by a loss arising from the            內 經 營 溢 利 有 所 增 加，惟 在 金 價 大 幅 波 動 下，有 關 增 幅
     revaluation of gold loan contracts amid volatility in gold prices             被黃金借貸合約重估虧損抵銷。
     during the period.


Gross profit margin                                                              毛利率
Reclassification of gold loan impact                                             黃金借貸影響的重新分類
• We use gold loans (short position in gold) for economic hedging                • 我們利用黃金借貸 ( 黃金淡倉 ) 為經濟避險目的，以減輕
     purpose to mitigate the financial impact of the gold price                    黃金存貨 ( 黃金好倉 ) 受黃金價格波動的財務影響。長線
     fluctuations on our gold inventories (long position). In the long-            而 言，預 期 黃 金 好、淡 倉 會 隨 著 黃 金 存 貨 出 售 而 相 互 抵
     term, the long and short positions in gold are expected to offset             銷 ；惟 短 線 而 言，未 平 倉 的 黃 金 借 貸 合 約 於 每 個 財 政 報
     each other, through the sales of gold inventories over time.                  告 期 末 或 於 合 約 平 倉 後 按 市 場 價 格 重 估，而遵照會計準
     However, in the near term, the outstanding gold loan contracts are            則，黃 金 存 貨 則 不 予 重 估。此 差 別 會 造 成 暫 時 的 會 計 收
     revalued at market price at the end of each financial reporting               益或虧損。
     period or upon its settlement, while the gold inventories are not, in
     accordance with accounting standards. This disparity has given to
     an accounting gain or loss temporarily.


• In the past, such gain or loss resulting from revaluation of gold              • 以 往，因 重 估 黃 金 借 貸 而 產 生 的 相 關 收 益 或虧損均入賬
     loans were accounted for in our cost of goods sold (“COGS”),                  至我們的「銷售成本」，並對我們所呈報的毛利及經營溢
     impacting our reported gross profit and operating profit. To better           利 帶 來 影 響。為 了 更 妥 善 地 反 映 不 受 對 沖 活動影響下的
     reflect our core underlying operational performance without the               核心營運表現，自 2025 財政年度起，我們現將黃金借貸
     impact of the hedging activities, we are reclassifying gold loan              重估收益或虧損由「銷售成本」重新分類為「其他收益及
     revaluation gain or loss from “COGS” to “other gains and losses”              虧損」。
     starting from FY2025.

56        Management Discussion and Analysis                                                                                                         Chow Tai Fook Jewellery Group Limited
          管理層討論與分析                                                                                                                                                 周大福珠寶集團有限公司
• We principally meet our working capital and other liquidity               • 我 們 主 要 透 過 經 營 所 得 現 金 流 量、銀 行 貸 款、黃 金 借 貸
   requirements through a combination of capital contributions,               及可換股債券的資本來源應付營運資金及其他流動資金
   including cash flows from operations, bank borrowings, gold loans          需 求。黃 金 借 貸 亦 用 作 經 濟 避 險 目 的 以減輕本集團黃金
   and convertible bonds. Gold loans are also used for economic               存貨受價格波動的財務影響。
   hedge purpose to mitigate the financial impact of price
   fluctuations in the Group’s gold inventories.


• The Group’s daily operations were mainly financed by operating cash       • 本 集 團 日 常 業 務 所 需 資 金 主 要 來 自 經 營現金流量，我們
   flows, and we mainly relied on short-term borrowings to satisfy            主要以短期借貸滿足旺季時的存貨資金需求及未來擴展
   inventory financing needs during peak seasons, as well as working          計 劃 所 需 營 運 資 金 及 預 計 之 外 的 需 求。本集團於償還貸
   capital for future expansion plans and unexpected needs. The Group         款方面並無任何困難。
   has not experienced any difficulties in repaying its borrowings.


• Details of the convertible bonds are set out in the section               • 可換股債券的詳情載於「其他資料」一節及簡明綜合財務
   "Additional Information" and note 20 to the condensed consolidated         報表附註 20。
   financial statements.


• The Group’s income and expenditure were mostly denominated in             • 本 集 團 之 收 入 及 開 支 主 要 以 港 元 及 人 民幣計值，而資產
   HKD and RMB, while its assets and liabilities were mostly                  及 負 債 則 主 要 以 港 元、人 民 幣 及 美 元 計 值。由 於 我 們 內
   denominated in HKD, RMB and USD. No hedging instrument is                  地 日 常 業 務 營 運 的 收 支 大 多 以 人 民 幣 結算，對我們的業
   deployed against RMB fluctuation as most daily receipts and                務 並 無 構 成 重 大 的 貨 幣 風 險，因 而 未 有針對人民幣波動
   payments for our Mainland operations are both made in RMB, which           動用任何對沖工具。
   do not pose a substantive currency exposure to our business.


• It is our treasury policy to maintain high liquidity in response to the   • 我 們 的 庫 務 政 策 旨 在 保 持 較 高 的 流 動 資金，以應付營運
   requirement of operating cash flows; and to uphold financial               現 金 流 量 的 需 求 ；同 時 緊 守 審 慎 的 財 務 政 策，避 免 涉 及
   prudence by not engaging in highly leveraged or speculative                高槓桿或投機性衍生產品。
   derivative products.




Interim Report 2025/2026                                                                              Other Financial Review   57
2025/2026 中期報告                                                                                                其他財務回顧
Effect of RMB fluctuation                                                      人民幣波動的影響
• As part of our business operation was in the Mainland, the                   • 由 於 我 們 有 部 分 業 務 在 內 地 經 營，人 民 幣 的波動對我們
     fluctuation in the RMB would post some impact on our performance.            的表現構成若干影響。


• Transactions entered by the Hong Kong entities but denominated in            • 香港公司以人民幣計值的交易 ( 包括與內地附屬公司進
     RMB, including the intra-group transactions with the Mainland                行的集團間交易 ) 會按交易當天的現貨匯率兌換為港元，
     subsidiaries, are converted into HKD, the functional currency of the         即 本 集 團 的 功 能 貨 幣，而 未 結 算 的 交 易 則 會以收市匯率
     Group, initially using the spot rate at the date of the transaction and      於 結 算 日 換 算。現 貨 匯 率 與 收 市 匯 率 的 換 算收益或虧損
     the unsettled transactions are retranslated at closing exchange rate         於 損 益 賬 中 確 認，而 人 民 幣 貶 值 會 對 我 們 的年內溢利構
     at the balance sheet date. Such conversion differences between the           成負面影響。
     spot rate and closing exchange rate are recognised in profit or loss,
     negatively affecting our profit for the year when the RMB
     depreciated.


• Exchange difference also arises when i) incomes and expenses of              • 此 外，匯 兌 差 異 源 於 i) 中 國 內 地 分 部 的 收 支 按 平 均 匯 率
     the Chinese Mainland segment are translated into HKD, the                    換 算 為 港 元，即 本 集 團 呈 列 貨 幣，而 相 應 的 資 產 及 負 債
     presentation currency of the Group, at the average exchange rate,            按收市匯率換算 ；及 ii) 中國內地分部的資產淨值於本財
     while the corresponding assets and liabilities are translated at             政年度的收市匯率較上個財政年度的收市匯率有所變動。
     closing exchange rate and ii) change in closing exchange rates at            有關匯兌差異於其他全面收益內確認入賬。
     the current financial year of the net assets of the Chinese
     Mainland segment from the closing rates at the previous financial
     year. Such translation differences are recognised in other
     comprehensive income.

SCOPE OF REVIEW                                                            審閱範圍
We conducted our review in accordance with International Standard          我 們 已 根 據 國 際 審 閱 準 則 第 2410 號「由 實 體 的 獨 立 核 數 師
on Review Engagements 2410, “Review of Interim Financial Information       執行中期財務資料審閱」進行審閱。審閱中期財務資料包括
Performed by the Independent Auditor of the Entity”. A review of           主 要 向 負 責 財 務 和 會 計 事 務 的 人 員 作 出 查 詢，及 應 用 分 析
interim financial information consists of making inquiries, primarily of   性 和 其 他 審 閱 程 序。審 閱 的 範 圍 遠 較 根 據《國 際 審 計 準 則》
persons responsible for financial and accounting matters, and applying     進 行 審 計 的 範 圍 為 小，故 不 能 令 我 們 可 保 證 我 們 將 知 悉 在
analytical and other review procedures. A review is substantially less     審計中可能被發現的所有重大事項。因此，我們不會發表審
in scope than an audit conducted in accordance with International          計意見。
Standards on Auditing and consequently does not enable us to obtain
assurance that we would become aware of all significant matters that
might be identified in an audit. Accordingly, we do not express an
audit opinion.

1.    GENERAL INFORMATION                                                 1.    一般資料
      Chow Tai Fook Jewellery Group Limited (the “Company”, and its             周大福珠寶集團有限公司 (「 本公司」及其附屬公司，
      subsidiaries collectively referred to as the “Group”) was                 統稱「本集團」) 於 2011 年 7 月 20 日根據開曼群島公司
      incorporated as an exempted company in the Cayman Islands                 法於開曼群島註冊成立為獲豁免有限公司。其股份自
      with limited liability under the Companies Law of the Cayman              2011 年 12 月 15 日開始於香港聯合交易所有限公司 (「聯
      Islands on 20 July 2011. Its shares were listed on the Main               交所」) 主板上市。其直接控股公司及最終控股公司為
      Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock              Chow Tai Fook Capital Limited，一 家 於 英 屬 維 爾 京
      Exchange”) since 15 December 2011. Its immediate holding                  群島註冊成立的公司。
      company and ultimate holding company is Chow Tai Fook
      Capital Limited, a company incorporated in the British
      Virgin Islands.

      The Company acts as an investment holding company. The                    本公司為投資控股公司。本集團的核心業務為生產及
      Group’s core business is manufacturing and selling of high-end            銷售名貴珠寶、主流珠寶及年青珠寶產品 ( 包括定價
      luxury, mass luxury and youth line jewellery products (including          首飾與計價黃金首飾 ) ，以及分銷不同品牌之鐘錶。
      fixed-price jewellery and weight-based gold jewellery), and               本公司的註冊辦事處地址為 Cricket Square, Hutchins
      distributing watches of various brands. The address of                    Drive, P.O. Box 2681, Grand Cayman KY1-1111,
      registered office is Cricket Square, Hutchins Drive, P.O. Box             Cayman Islands。 主 要 營 業 地 址 為 香 港 皇 后 大 道 中
      2681, Grand Cayman KY1-1111, Cayman Islands. The address of               16–18 號新世界大廈 33 樓。
      principal place of business is 33/F, New World Tower, 16–18
      Queen’s Road Central, Hong Kong.

      The condensed consolidated financial statements are presented             簡明綜合財務報表以港元 ( 即本公司的功能貨幣 ) 呈
      in Hong Kong dollars (“HK$”), which is the same as the                    列，並 已 根 據 聯 交 所 證 券 上 市 規 則 (「 上 市 規 則」) 附
      functional currency of the Company and has been prepared in               錄 16 的適用披露規定以及國際會計準則第 34 號「中期
      accordance with the applicable disclosure requirements of                 財務報告」編製。
      Appendix 16 of the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on
      the Stock Exchange (the “Listing Rules”) and with International
      Accounting Standard (“IAS”) 34 “Interim financial reporting”.


2.    ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                                 2.    會計政策
      The condensed consolidated financial statements have been                 除若干金融工具及負債按公允值計量外，簡明綜合財
      prepared on the historical cost basis except that certain                 務報表按歷史成本基準並根據國際財務報告準則編製。
      financial instruments and liabilities, which are measured at fair
      values, and in accordance with the IFRS Accounting Standards.

      The accounting policies adopted are consistent with those set             所 採 納 的 會 計 政 策 與 本 集 團 截 至 2025 年 3 月 31 日 止
      out in the Group’s annual consolidated financial statements for           年度的年度綜合財務報表所載述的會計政策一致，唯
      the year ended 31 March 2025, except for the adoption of                  一例外是採納下文所載之經修訂準則及可換股債券的
      amended standards and the accounting policies of convertible              會計政策。若干準則的修訂本已經發布並自 2025 年 4
      bonds as set out below. A number of amendments to standards               月 1 日起生效。本集團於本期間首次採用該等修訂本，
      have been issued and are effective from 1 April 2025. The                 惟這對於本集團中期財務報表並無構成重大影響。
      Group applies these amendments for the first time in the
      current period, but do not have a material impact on the interim
      financial statements of the Group.

2.   ACCOUNTING POLICIES (CONTINUED)                                      2.   會計政策 ( 續 )
     Convertible bond                                                          可換股債券
     The component of convertible bonds that exhibits                          可換股債券具有負債特徵的部分，於扣除交易成本後
     characteristics of a liability is recognised as a liability in the        在 綜 合 財 務 狀 況 表 內 確 認 為 負 債。 發 行 可 換 股 債 券
     consolidated statement of financial position, net of transaction          時，負債部分的公允值乃根據同等不可換股債券之市
     costs. On issuance of convertible bonds, the fair value of the            場利率而釐定，而該數額按攤銷成本基準列作長期負
     liability component is determined using a market rate for an              債，直至債券獲兌換或贖回為止。所得款餘額於扣除
     equivalent non-convertible bond; and this amount is carried as            交易成本後，將分配至已確認並計入股東權益之換股
     a long-term liability on the amortised cost basis until                   權內。換股權的賬面值於其後年度將不會重新計量。
     extinguished on conversion or redemption. The remainder of the            交易成本根據工具初始確認時所得款分配至負債與權
     proceeds is allocated to the conversion option that is                    益部分的情況，攤分至可換股債券的負債與權益部分。
     recognised and included in shareholders’ equity, net of
     transaction costs. The carrying amount of the conversion option
     is not remeasured in subsequent years. Transaction costs are
     apportioned between the liability and equity components of
     the convertible bonds based on the allocation of proceeds to
     the liability and equity components when the instruments are
     first recognised.


     If the early redemption option of convertible bonds exhibits              倘可換股債券的提早換股權顯示嵌入式衍生工具的特
     characteristics of an embedded derivative, it is separated from           徵，則與其負債部分分開。衍生工具部分的確認是基
     its liability component. The derivative component is recognised           於整個負債部分的公允值與債務部分公允值之間的差
     as the difference between the fair value of the liability                 額。可換股債券的衍生工具部分在初始和隨後計量時
     component as a whole and the fair value of the debt                       均按公允值計量，並作為衍生金融工具的一部分呈列。
     component. The derivative component of the convertible bonds
     is measured initially and subsequently at fair value and
     presented as part of derivative financial instruments.


3.   REVENUE AND SEGMENT INFORMATION                                      3.   營業額及分部資料
     Revenue represents the net amounts received and receivable                營業額指已收及應收售出商品及提供服務的款項減退
     for goods sold and services provided less returns and net of              貨及交易折扣的淨額。
     trade discounts.


     For the purposes of resource allocation and performance                   就資源分配及表現評估而向主要營運決策者 ( 即本公
     assessment, information reported to the chief operating                   司執行董事 ) 報告的資料主要按管理所在地劃分。主
     decision maker (the “CODM”), which comprises executive                    要營運決策者在審閱時會進一步將各管理所在地所得
     directors of the Company, mainly focuses on the location of               營 業 額 按 零 售 及 批 發 市 場 劃 分 進 行 分 析。 截 至 2025
     management. Revenue derived from each location of                         年 及 2024 年 9 月 30 日 止 六 個 月， 本 集 團 的 可 呈 報 營
     management is further analysed into those from retail and                 運分部包括兩個分部，即 (i) 位於中國內地的業務及 (ii)
     wholesale markets when reviewed by CODM. The Group’s                      位於中國香港、中國澳門及其他市場的業務。
     reportable and operating segments for the six months ended
     30 September 2025 and 2024 included two segments, namely
     (i) business in the Chinese Mainland and (ii) business in Hong
     Kong & Macau of China and other markets.

This interim report is printed on FSC ® Certified and other controlled material. Pulps used are
chlorine-free and acid-free. The FSC ® logo identifies products which contain wood from well-managed
forests certified in accordance with the rules of the Forest Stewardship Council® .
此中期報告由 FSC ® 認證的和其他受控來源的材料印製。紙漿無氯氣漂染及不含酸性。FSC ® 標誌表示產品所含
的木料來自管理良好的森林；該等森林根據 Forest Stewardship Council ® 的規例獲得認可。
